Whether the Native, or Querent may bave Children by
the Figure. |
- Humane Signs'on the Cuſp of the firſt and fifth I *
Houſes, Saturn ſtrong, Lord of the fifth, in Trine k
- unto Jupiter, one argument of Children. Second-
ly, the Moon and 4 way are both in Trine to the
Part of Fortune. Thirdly, the Moon is in Trine
unto Zapiter and the Sun. Fourthly, Saturn, Lord
of the fifth, is within three degrees of the Cuſp,
all which are arguments of Children. There is
but one teſtimony againſt it, which is the Quartile
of Sararn and Venus ;, but in regard Venas is a For- }
tune, and ſtrong in his Honſe, it will not hinder
conception ; however, Pluralities of Teſtimonies
muſt prevail. Generally, Signs fruitful on the Cuſps
of the firſt and fifth Houſes, a Fortune in the fifth,
the Lord of the fifth ſtrong: in Trine t++ Fupirer, or
-- unto Part of Fortune; the Moon in Trine to Fupi-
ter, Venus, or Part of Fortune; the Lord of the
fifth ſtrong in the Aſcendant, or the Lord of the 4-
Tendant ſtrong in the fifth, or if Part of Fortune
be ſtrong in the fifth, all theſe are arguments of
Children, But if the Signs on the Cuſps of the
firſt and fifth Houſes be barren Signs, or if the fifth
Hoſe be afflited by. the maleyolent Planets, or if
the Lord of the fifth, or Part of Fortune be aflicted
by the Infortunes, or if the principal Significators |
be in bad Houſes, and evilly- aſpedted, or under
comuſiion, it argueth no Children.
